> DEVELOPER

对话：跨 Feature 身份标识统一讨论

  Lane B: 跨 Feature 数据身份标识统一讨论

  ## 目标
  审查 frame / receive / storage / display 四个 feature 之间的数据身份标识和映射关系，判断：
  1. 当前有多少种标识模型，各自解决什么问题
  2. 哪些映射是必要的，哪些是过度设计
  3. 是否存在更简单、更统一的标识方案
  4. 给出具体结论：统一为一种 / 保留两种 / 维持现状（每条结论必须有代码证据支撑）

  ## 直接合同
  - rewrite/src/features/frame/（帧定义，标识源头）
  - rewrite/src/features/receive/（解析产出，做第一次映射）
  - rewrite/src/features/storage/（持久化，自己的 channel 标识）
  - rewrite/src/features/display/ 或 rewrite/src/features/visualization/（展示，groupId + dataItemId）

  ## 边界护栏
  - CLAUDE.md（selector 不可变约束、feature 归口规则）
  - codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-boundaries.md
  - codestable/features/rewrite-display/rewrite-display-ui-design.md（display 侧已锁定的设计）
  - codestable/features/rewrite-storage-local-baseline/（storage 侧已锁定的设计）

  ## 子 agent 派发策略

  ### 批次 1：事实收集（4-5 个 explore agent 并行）

  **Agent 1：Frame 标识模型**
  - 读 frame feature 的 core types 和 selectors
  - 提取：frameId、fieldId、direction 等标识字段的定义和使用方式
  - 这是标识链的源头，后续所有映射都从这里出发

  **Agent 2：Receive 映射层**
  - 读 receive feature 的 processor、selectors、types
  - 提取：从帧解析结果到下游消费的每一步映射
  - 特别关注：ReadModel、ReceiveEvent、MatchedField 等中间类型
  - 列出每个映射函数的输入类型 → 输出类型 → 映射逻辑

  **Agent 3：Storage 标识模型**
  - 读 storage feature 的 core types、selectors
  - 提取：channel、record 等标识的定义
  - 特别关注：从 receive 数据到 storage record 的转换在哪做、怎么映射

  **Agent 4：Display 标识模型**
  - 读 display/visualization feature 的 types、selectors
  - 提取：groupId、dataItemId 等标识的定义
  - 特别关注：从 receive 数据到展示数据的转换在哪做、怎么映射

  **Agent 5：跨 feature 边界映射汇总**
  - 搜索所有 import 另一个 feature 类型的代码
  - 提取：feature 间实际传递的数据类型和转换点
  - 画出完整的标识转换链路图

  ### 批次 2：分析（2-3 个 agent）

  **Agent 6：映射复杂度审计**
  - 输入：批次 1 所有 agent 的产出
  - 对每个映射点问三个问题：
    1. 这个映射解决什么问题？（去掉会怎样）
    2. 映射逻辑有多复杂？（一行赋值 vs 十行转换 vs 需要查表）
    3. 映射是否有信息损失？（映射后能否反推回原始标识）
  - 输出：每个映射点的复杂度评分和必要性判断

  **Agent 7：简化方案探索**
  - 输入：批次 1 所有 agent 的产出
  - 设计 2-3 个备选简化方案：
    - 方案 A：统一标识（frameId+fieldId 一到底，display/storage 用同一套）
    - 方案 B：双标识（定义标识 frameId+fieldId + 运行时标识，各 feature 内部自由组织但对外用运行时标识）
    - 方案 C：维持现状但精简（去掉不必要的中间映射）
  - 每个方案列出：优势、代价、改动范围

  **Agent 8：其他项目经验**
  - 搜索参考项目 refactor/ 中是否有类似的身份标识设计
  - 搜索 rewrite/src/shared/ 是否已有标识相关的工具函数
  - 看是否有可以直接复用的模式

  ### 批次 3：自检（2 个 agent）

  **Agent 9：反向验证**
  - 对每个简化方案，模拟以下场景验证：
    1. 同一参数在表格、图表、存储中是否仍可关联
    2. 删除帧后，已有存储数据是否仍可追溯
    3. 多连接场景下是否有标识冲突
    4. 收藏/筛选等用户操作是否仍可工作
  - 每个场景标注：方案可行 / 方案有风险 / 方案不可行

  **Agent 10：影响面评估**
  - 对推荐的简化方案，列出实际需要改的文件清单
  - 评估：是现在改（display 实施前）还是可以后续改
  - 标注：哪些已锁定设计需要更新

  ---

  ## 产出要求

  1. **brainstorm 文档**：codestable/features/rewrite-display/display-identity-brainstorm.md
     - 事实层：当前标识模型全景 + 每个映射点的详细分析
     - 决策层：推荐方案 + 原因 + 代价
     - 自检层：R1 证据、R2 覆盖、R3 反向

  2. **结论**：明确回答以下问题：
     - 当前有多少个映射转换点？列出每个
     - 推荐统一还是维持现状？
     - 如果统一，用什么标识模型？
     - 需要改动哪些已锁定设计？
     - 实施时机：现在（display 实施前）还是之后？

  ## 硬约束

  - 只讨论标识和映射，不重新讨论 display/storage/receive 的业务逻辑
  - 结论必须有代码证据，不接受纯理论推导
  - 不推翻已通过验收的 feature 核心设计（frame/receive/send/task）
  - 可以建议修改已锁定设计的非核心部分（如 selector 返回类型、中间映射函数），但必须说明影响面
  - 这是设计讨论，不实现代码

Based on my comprehensive exploration of the codebase, I've identified all cross-feature imports and data flow patterns. Here's the complete dependency map:

## Cross-Feature Import Analysis

### FEATURE DEPENDENCY MAP

#### 1. **frame** (Bottom layer - no outgoing dependencies)
The `frame` feature is a foundational type provider - it only exports types, no incoming imports from other features.

**Exports used by other features:**
- `FrameAsset`, `ReadonlyFrameAsset`, `FrameFieldDefinition`
- `FrameAssetReader`, `ExpressionVariableDefinition`, `ExpressionDefinition`, `ChecksumMethod`
- `IdentifierRule`, `ReadonlyDeep`, `FrameDataType`

---

#### 2. **receive** → imports from **frame** and **connection**

**Import statements:**
```typescript
// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/receive/core/types.ts
import type {
  ExpressionVariableDefinition,
  FrameAsset,
  FrameDataType,
  ReadonlyFrameAsset,
} from '@/features/frame';

import type {
  CompiledConditional,
  VariableValue,
} from '@/shared/expression';

import type {
  TransportErrorKind,
  TransportKind,
} from '@/features/connection';
```

**Usage:**
- Uses `FrameAsset`, `ReadonlyFrameAsset` for frame matching and parsing
- Uses `ExpressionVariableDefinition` for expression evaluation
- Uses `TransportErrorKind`, `TransportKind` for transport layer integration

---

#### 3. **send** → imports from **frame** and **connection**

**Import statements:**
```typescript
// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/core/types.ts
export type { ExpressionDefinition, ChecksumMethod } from '@/features/frame';

// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/core/frame-resolver.ts
import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';

// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/adapters/ports.ts
import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
import type { TransportTargetSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
```

**Usage:**
- Re-exports `ExpressionDefinition`, `ChecksumMethod` from frame
- Uses `ReadonlyFrameAsset` for frame resolution
- Uses `TransportTargetSnapshot` for target resolution

---

#### 4. **task** → imports from **send** and **frame**

**Import statements:**
```typescript

// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/adapters/ports.ts

**Usage:**

#### 5. **command-ingress** → imports from **task**, **send**, **connection**, **frame**

**Import statements:**
```typescript

#### 6. **storage-local-baseline** and **display** (No cross-feature imports)

---

### RUNTIME ORCHESTRATION LAYER

The `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/` directory contains the actual data flow bridges:

#### **feature-wiring.ts** - Main dependency injection

```typescript
// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/feature-wiring.ts
import {
  createFrameAssetReader,
  type FrameAssetReader,
} from '@/features/frame';

import {
  createStorageLocalReader,
  createStorageLocalService,
  type StorageLocalReader,
  type StorageLocalService,
  type LocalMaterialAdapter,
} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';

import {
  createConnectionService,
  type ConnectionService,
  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
} from '@/features/connection';

import {
  createReceiveService,
  type ReceiveService,
} from '@/features/receive';

import {
  createSendService,
  type SendService,
} from '@/features/send';

import {
  createTaskService,
  type TaskService,
} from '@/features/task';

import {
  createDisplayService,
  type DisplayService,
} from '@/features/display';
```

**Wiring hierarchy:**
- **L0**: frameReader, settingsService, storageReader (no dependencies)
- **L1**: connectionService (needs adapter)
- **L2**: receiveService, sendService (need L0 + L1)
- **L3**: taskService, displayService (need L2)
- **L4**: command-ingress (optional, needs L0-L3)

---

#### **routing-tick.ts** - Main data flow orchestration

**Import statements:**
```typescript
// /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/routing-tick.ts
import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '@/features/task';
import { ConnectionToReceiveInputSource } from './bridges/connection-to-receive';
import { fanOutToDisplay } from './bridges/receive-display-bridge';
import { fanOutToStorage } from './bridges/receive-storage-bridge';
import type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
```

**Data flow chain:**
1. `ConnectionService` → `TransportEventSnapshot`
2. `ConnectionToReceiveInputSource` → `ReceiveInputEvent`
3. `ReceiveService` → `ReceiveBatchOutcome`
4. `ReceiveBatchOutcome` → multiple fan-outs:
   - `ConditionMatchInput` → `TaskService` (via `receiveEventSourceBridge`)
   - `DisplayFieldMaterial` → `DisplayService` (via `fanOutToDisplay`)
   - `StorageLocalRecord` → `StorageLocalService` (via `fanOutToStorage`)

---

#### **Bridge files** - Type mapping between features

**receive-storage-bridge.ts:**
```typescript
import type { StorageLocalService, StorageLocalRecord } from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
import type { ReceiveBatchOutcome } from '@/features/receive';

// Maps ReceiveBatchOutcome → StorageLocalRecord
function toStorageRecord(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): StorageLocalRecord | undefined
```

**receive-display-bridge.ts:**
```typescript
import type { DisplayService, DisplayFieldMaterial } from '@/features/display';
import type { ReceiveBatchOutcome } from '@/features/receive';

// Maps ReceiveBatchOutcome → DisplayFieldMaterial
export function fanOutToDisplay(
  service: DisplayService,
  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[],
): number
```

**connection-to-receive.ts:**
```typescript
import type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
import type { ReceiveInputSource, ReceiveInputEvent } from '@/features/receive';

// Maps TransportEventSnapshot → ReceiveInputEvent
export class ConnectionToReceiveInputSource implements ReceiveInputSource
```

---

### COMPLETE DATA FLOW CHAIN

```
[Connection Transport]
         ↓
[TransportEventSnapshot] (connection feature)
         ↓
[ConnectionToReceiveInputSource] (runtime bridge)
         ↓
[ReceiveInputEvent] (receive feature)
         ↓
[ReceiveService.ingestBatch]
         ↓
[ReceiveBatchOutcome] (receive feature)
         ↓
    ┌────┴────┬────────────┐
    ↓         ↓            ↓
[Task]    [Display]    [Storage]
    ↓         ↓            ↓
[Condition  [Display    [Storage
 MatchInput] Field]     LocalRecord]
    ↓         ↓            ↓
[Send       [Display    [Append
 Request]   Service]   Records]
    ↓
[SendService.execute]
    ↓
[SendResult]
```

---

### SHARED TYPES

The `@/shared` directory contains utilities used across features:
- `@/shared/expression` - Expression compilation/evaluation
- `@/shared/types/readonly-deep` - Deep readonly types
- `@/shared` - Date utilities

These are NOT feature-specific and are used by multiple features without creating feature boundaries.

Based on my thorough exploration of the receive feature's mapping layer, I can now provide you with a structured summary showing the exact type definitions and mapping flows.

## RECEIVE FEATURE MAPPING LAYER ANALYSIS

### 1. TYPE DEFINITIONS IN `/rewrite/src/features/receive/core/types.ts`

#### Core Mapping Types

```typescript
// INPUT TYPE: Raw incoming data from transport layer
export interface ReceiveInputBatch {
  readonly id: string;                    // Batch identifier
  readonly bytes: readonly number[];      // Raw byte array
  readonly receivedAt: string;            // ISO timestamp
  readonly source: ReceiveSourceSnapshot; // Source metadata
  readonly sequence?: number;             // Optional sequence number
  readonly referenceVersion?: number;     // Frame reference version
}

// INPUT TYPE: Error from transport layer
export interface ReceiveInputError {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly occurredAt: string;
  readonly kind: TransportErrorKind | 'invalid-input' | 'stale-reference' | 'unknown';
  readonly message: string;
  readonly source?: ReceiveSourceSnapshot;
  readonly recoverable?: boolean;
  readonly referenceVersion?: number;
}

// INTERMEDIATE TYPE: Parsed field with metadata
export interface ReceiveParsedFieldValue {
  readonly frameId: string;              // From frame feature
  readonly frameName: string;            // From frame feature
  readonly fieldId: string;              // From frame feature
  readonly fieldName: string;            // From frame feature
  readonly dataType: FrameDataType;      // From frame feature
  readonly offset: number;               // Byte offset in frame
  readonly length: number;               // Field length in bytes
  readonly rawHex: string;               // Hex representation
  readonly value: ReceiveParsedFieldPrimitive;  // Parsed primitive value
  readonly displayValue: string;         // Formatted display value
  readonly label?: string;               // Optional label from options
  readonly expressionApplied: boolean;   // Whether expression was applied
  readonly expressionError?: string;     // Expression error if any
}

// INTERMEDIATE TYPE: Frame match summary
export interface ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary {
  readonly frameId: string;              // From frame feature
  readonly frameName: string;            // From frame feature
  readonly byteLength: number;           // Total frame length
  readonly fieldCount: number;           // Number of fields
}

// OUTPUT TYPE: Final field value with temporal metadata
export interface ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot extends ReceiveParsedFieldValue {
  readonly updatedAt: string;            // When this value was last updated
  readonly sourceId: string;             // Which source provided this
}
```

#### Processing Types

```typescript
// PROCESSING INPUT: All context needed for batch processing
export interface ReceiveProcessInput {
  readonly batch: ReceiveInputBatch;
  readonly reference: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot;
  readonly processedAt: string;
  readonly expressionCache?: ExpressionCompileCache;
  readonly readModel?: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>>;
}

// PROCESSING OUTPUT: Result of processing a batch
export interface ReceiveBatchOutcome {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: ReceiveOutcomeKind;
  readonly processedAt: string;
  readonly input?: ReceiveInputBatch;
  readonly matchedFrame?: ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary;
  readonly fields: readonly ReceiveParsedFieldValue[];
  readonly issues: readonly ReceiveIssue[];
  readonly statsDelta: ReceiveStatsDelta;
}

// STATE SNAPSHOT: Complete receive state
export interface ReceiveStateSnapshot {
  readonly schemaVersion: typeof RECEIVE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  readonly lifecycle: ReceiveLifecycleStatus;
  readonly referenceVersion: number;
  readonly counters: ReceiveCounterSnapshot;
  readonly frameStats: readonly ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot[];
  readonly sourceStats: readonly ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot[];
  readonly fieldValues: readonly ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[];
  readonly recentInputs: readonly ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot[];
  readonly events: readonly ReceiveEventSnapshot[];
  readonly currentFrame?: ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary;
  readonly lastIssue?: ReceiveIssue;
}
```

### 2. MAPPING FUNCTIONS IN `/rewrite/src/features/receive/core/processor.ts`

```typescript
// INPUT: ReceiveProcessInput → OUTPUT: ReceiveBatchOutcome
export function processReceiveBatch(input: ReceiveProcessInput): ReceiveBatchOutcome

// INPUT: ReceiveInputError → OUTPUT: ReceiveBatchOutcome
export function createReceiveInputErrorOutcome(
  error: ReceiveInputError,
  processedAt: string,
): ReceiveBatchOutcome

// INPUT: (id, batch, expectedVersion, processedAt) → OUTPUT: ReceiveBatchOutcome
export function createStaleReceiveInputOutcome(
  id: string,
  batch: ReceiveInputBatch,
  expectedReferenceVersion: number,
  processedAt: string,
): ReceiveBatchOutcome

// INPUT: readonly number[] → OUTPUT: string (hex display)

```

### 7. HOW FRAME/FRAMEID/FIELDID FROM FRAME FEATURE APPEARS IN RECEIVE TYPES

**Frame Feature Types (from `/rewrite/src/features/frame/core/types.ts`):**
```typescript
export interface FrameAsset {
  id: string;              // → ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary.frameId
  name: string;            // → ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary.frameName
  direction: FrameDirection; // Used to filter for 'receive'
  fields: FrameFieldDefinition[];
  identifierRules?: IdentifierRule[];
}

export interface FrameFieldDefinition {
  id: string;              // → ReceiveParsedFieldValue.fieldId
  name: string;            // → ReceiveParsedFieldValue.fieldName
  dataType: FrameDataType; // → ReceiveParsedFieldValue.dataType
  length: number;
}
```

**Receive Feature Uses Frame IDs Directly:**
- `ReceiveParsedFieldValue.frameId` - Direct reference to `FrameAsset.id`
- `ReceiveParsedFieldValue.fieldId` - Direct reference to `FrameFieldDefinition.id`
- `ReceiveMatchedFrameSummary.frameId` - Direct reference to `FrameAsset.id`
- `ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot.frames` - Array of `ReadonlyFrameAsset[]`

### 8. NEW IDENTIFIERS INTRODUCED BY RECEIVE

**Receive creates its own identifiers:**

```typescript
// Batch-level IDs
ReceiveInputBatch.id: string              // Unique batch identifier
ReceiveBatchOutcome.id: string            // Links to batch.id
ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot.id: string     // Links to outcome.id
ReceiveEventSnapshot.id: string           // Links to outcome.id

// Source IDs
ReceiveSourceSnapshot.sourceId: string    // From connection layer
ReceiveSourceSnapshot.connectionId: string

// State tracking IDs
ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot.referenceVersion: number  // Increments on refresh

// ReadModel keys (composite identifiers)
selectReadModelIndex returns: Map<frameId, Map<fieldId, VariableValue>>
// Key format: "${frameId}:${fieldId}" used in fieldKey() function
```

### 9. KEY MAPPING FLOW SUMMARY

```
Transport Layer
    ↓
ReceiveInputBatch (bytes + metadata)
    ↓
processReceiveBatch()
    ↓
[1] matchReceiveFrame() → ReceiveFrameMatchOutcome
    ↓
[2] parseReceiveFrameFields() → ReceiveParsedFieldValue[]
    ↓
[3] evaluateFrameExpressions() → Updated ReceiveParsedFieldValue[]
    ↓
ReceiveBatchOutcome (with statsDelta)
    ↓
state.applyOutcome() → ReceiveStateSnapshot
    ↓
Selectors → UI/Downstream Consumers
```

**All mapping preserves frame/field identifiers** from the frame feature - receive does NOT create its own frame or field IDs. It only creates:
- Batch/outcome IDs for tracking
- Source IDs from connections
- Reference version for cache invalidation
- Composite keys for the read model index
```

// File: /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/core/validation-frame.ts
// Lines: 95-117

export function validateFrameAssetCollection(frames: readonly FrameAsset[]): ValidationResult {
  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
  const ids = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const [index, frame] of frames.entries()) {
    issues.push(...validateFrameAsset(frame, `frames.${index}`).issues);
    const id = frame.id.trim();
    if (!id) continue;
    ids.set(id, (ids.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  for (const [id, count] of ids.entries()) {
    if (count > 1) {
      issues.push(createIssue('frame.idDuplicate', 'frames', `帧 ID 必须唯一: ${id}`));
    }
  }
  return toResult(issues);
}
```

**Usage in Selectors:**
```typescript
// File: /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/selectors/frame-selectors.ts

export function getFrameAsset(
  source: FrameAssetSource,
  frameId: string,
): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined {
  const frame = getFrames(source).find((item) => item.id === frameId);
  return frame ? cloneFrameAsset(frame) : undefined;
}

// File: /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/selectors/frame-selectors.ts
// Lines: 148-172

export function selectFieldReferenceOptions(
  source: FrameAssetSource,
  query: FrameFieldReferenceQuery = {},
): FrameFieldReference[] {
  return getFrames(source)
    .filter((frame) => {
      if (query.frameId && frame.id !== query.frameId) {  // ← Filter by frameId
        return false;
      }
      return query.direction ? frame.direction === query.direction : true;
    })
    .flatMap((frame) =>
      frame.fields.map((field) => ({
        frameId: frame.id,  // ← Reference frame ID
        frameName: frame.name,
        direction: frame.direction,
        fieldId: field.id,
        fieldName: field.name,
        dataType: field.dataType,
        inputType: field.inputType,
        dataParticipationType: field.dataParticipationType,
      })),
    );
}
```

// File: /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/state/frame-state.ts
// Lines: 57-68

upsertFrame(frame) {
  const nextFrame = cloneFrameAsset(frame);
  const index = frames.findIndex((item) => item.id === frame.id);  // ← Find by ID

  if (index === -1) {
    frames = [...frames, nextFrame];
  } else {
    frames = frames.map((item, itemIndex) => (itemIndex === index ? nextFrame : item));
  }
  return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
}

// Lines: 70-77
removeFrame(frameId) {
  frames = frames.filter((frame) => frame.id !== frameId);  // ← Filter by ID
  if (selectedFrameId === frameId) {
    selectedFrameId = undefined;
  }
  return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
}
```

**What it identifies:** A **frame template** - the schema/definition of a protocol frame (e.g., "pilot-frame-001"). This is a template ID, not a runtime instance ID.

---

### **4. COMPLETE IDENTIFIER SUMMARY TABLE**

| Identifier | Type | Location | Uniqueness | What It Identifies |
|------------|------|----------|------------|-------------------|
| `frame.id` | `string` | `FrameAsset.id` | **Globally unique** across all frames | A frame template definition |
| `field.id` | `string` | `FrameFieldDefinition.id` | **Unique within frame** | A field definition within a frame |
| `frameId` | `string` | `ExpressionVariableDefinition.frameId` | Reference to `FrameAsset.id` | Target frame for field reference |
| `fieldId` | `string` | `ExpressionVariableDefinition.fieldId` | Reference to `FrameFieldDefinition.id` | Target field for expression variable |
| `selectedFrameId` | `string \| undefined` | `FrameStateSnapshot.selectedFrameId` | Single value | Currently active frame in UI |
| `direction` | `'send' \| 'receive'` | `FrameAsset.direction` | Enum value | Communication direction of frame |

---

### **5. KEY FINDINGS**

1. **No Composite Keys**: The system does NOT use composite keys like `FrameKey` or `FieldKey`. Identification is done through:
   - Simple string IDs (`frame.id`, `field.id`)
   - Reference pairs (`frameId + fieldId` in expressions)

2. **Scope of Uniqueness**:
   - `frame.id`: Must be unique across ALL frames (enforced by validation)
   - `field.id`: Must be unique within a single frame (enforced by field name uniqueness check)

3. **Reference Pattern**: When fields reference other fields (in expressions), they use the **pair** `frameId + fieldId` to uniquely identify the target field across the entire frame collection.

4. **State vs. Template**: `selectedFrameId` is runtime state (which frame is currently selected), while `frame.id` and `field.id` are template identifiers (schema definitions).

5. **Direction as Filter**: `direction` is not part of the identity but is used extensively as a filter/query parameter to categorize frames by their communication purpose.

### 2. HOW DISPLAY RECEIVES DATA

#### From Receive Feature (Real-time Data Flow)

**Bridge Function:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/bridges/receive-display-bridge.ts`

```typescript
// Lines 4-30: Identity mapping from receive to display
export function fanOutToDisplay(
  service: DisplayService,
  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[],
): number {
  const fields: DisplayFieldMaterial[] = [];

  for (const outcome of outcomes) {
    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;
    if (outcome.fields.length === 0) continue;

    for (const f of outcome.fields) {
      fields.push({
        groupId: f.frameId,        // CRITICAL: frameId → groupId mapping
        dataItemId: f.fieldId,     // CRITICAL: fieldId → dataItemId mapping
        fieldName: f.fieldName,
        value: f.value,
        displayValue: f.displayValue,
        updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,
      });
    }
  }

  if (fields.length === 0) return 0;
  const result = service.ingestSourceMaterial({ fields });
  return result.ok ? fields.length : 0;
}
```

**Receive Feature Structure:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/receive/core/types.ts`

```typescript
// Lines 80-92: Receive feature uses different identifier names

  readonly id: string;              // fieldId in receive
  readonly name: string;            // fieldName
  readonly dataType: string;
  readonly offset: number;
  readonly length: number;
  // ...
}

export interface ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot {
  readonly frameId: string;         // Maps to display.groupId
  readonly fieldId: string;         // Maps to display.dataItemId
  readonly fieldName: string;
  readonly value: unknown;
  readonly displayValue: string;
  // ...
}
```

### 3. DISPLAY SERVICE DATA INGESTION

**Service Implementation:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/services/display-service.ts`

```typescript
// Lines 54-57: Internal buffer structure
interface DisplayBuffer {
  chartHistory: Map<string, ChartPoint[]>;  // Key: "groupId:dataItemId"
  sourceFields: DisplayFieldMaterial[];
}

// Lines 145-169: Data ingestion method
ingestSourceMaterial(material) {
  const current = state.getSnapshot();

  if (material.fields) {
    buffer.sourceFields = material.fields.map((f) => ({ ...f }));
  }

  if (material.availability) {
    const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
      buffer,
      current.preferences,
      material.availability,
      state,
    );
    return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
  }

  const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
    buffer,
    current.preferences,
    current.availability,
    state,
  );
  return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
}
```

### 4. DESIGN DOCUMENT IDENTIFIER ANALYSIS

**Design Document:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/features/rewrite-display/rewrite-display-ui-design.md`

**Lines 451-467: Identity Model Crisis Documented**

```markdown
## 10. Storage → Display 转换（需新对话确认身份模型）

**⚠️ 此节依赖数据身份标识统一讨论**：当前 frame(`frameId+fieldId`)、display(`groupId+dataItemId`)、storage(`channel`) 三方标识不统一。需要新对话讨论是否可以简化为一个统一模型。当前设计作为临时方案，讨论后可能重构。

临时方案：

```typescript
// display/core/storage-convert.ts（临时）
export interface StorageConvertContext {
  resolveChannelIdentity(channel: string): { groupId: string; dataItemId: string };
}

export function storageRecordsToDisplayMaterials(
  records: readonly StorageLocalRecord[],
  context: StorageConvertContext
): DisplayFieldMaterial[]
```

如果身份模型统一后，此函数可能大幅简化或不再需要。
```

**Brainstorm Document:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/features/rewrite-display/rewrite-display-ui-brainstorm.md`

**Lines 444-458: Three-Way Identity Mismatch**

```markdown
## D13: 数据身份标识统一（需新对话讨论）

**现状**：三个 feature 各用不同身份标识：
- frame: `frameId + fieldId` — 帧定义和字段定义
- display: `groupId + dataItemId` — 运行时分组和数据项
- storage: `channel` — 单一字符串

这导致 storage→display 转换需要一个"映射函数"，本质是身份模型不统一的问题。当前设计用一个 resolveChannelIdentity context 参数绕过，但这是设计债务。

**决策**：标记为 **需新对话专门讨论**。目标是用更简单、更统一的结构替代当前的三方身份标识。可能的方向：
- 统一用 `frameId + fieldId` 作为跨 feature 的唯一标识
- storage 的 `channel` 改为结构化类型
- display 的 `groupId` 是否就是 `frameId`，`dataItemId` 是否就是 `fieldId`
```

  readonly selectedItems: readonly string[];  // Array of dataItemIds

### 7. PROJECTION AND SELECTOR PATTERNS

**Projection Logic:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/core/projection.ts`

```typescript
// Lines 14-29: Table row filtering by groupId and dataItemId
export function projectTableRows(
  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
  selectedGroupId: string,
  selectedItems: readonly string[],
): TableRowProjection[] {
  const filtered = selectedGroupId
    ? fields.filter((f) => f.groupId === selectedGroupId)
    : fields;

  if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
    return filtered.map(toRow);
  }

  const itemSet = new Set(selectedItems);
  return filtered.filter((f) => itemSet.has(f.dataItemId)).map(toRow);
}

// Lines 95-105: Scatter source binding by compound key
function findFieldByBinding(
  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
  binding: ScatterSourceBinding,
): DisplayFieldMaterial | undefined {
  if (!binding.groupId || !binding.dataItemId) {
    return undefined;
  }
  return fields.find(
    (f) => f.groupId === binding.groupId && f.dataItemId === binding.dataItemId,
  );
}
```

### SUMMARY OF IDENTIFIER MAPPING

| Feature | Group Identifier | Item Identifier | Compound Key Format |
|---------|-----------------|-----------------|---------------------|
| **Frame** | `frameId` | `fieldId` | `frameId:fieldId` (internal) |
| **Display** | `groupId` | `dataItemId` | `groupId:dataItemId` (chart history) |
| **Storage** | `channel` (single string) | N/A | N/A |
| **Bridge Mapping** | `frameId` → `groupId` | `fieldId` → `dataItemId` | `groupId:dataItemId` |

**CRITICAL FINDING:** The design documents explicitly identify this three-way identifier mismatch as "设计债务" (design debt) requiring a dedicated conversation to unify the identity model across features. The current implementation uses a bridge pattern in `fanOutToDisplay()` to map between different identifier namespaces.

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding of the storage feature's identity model. Let me create a comprehensive structured summary:

# Storage Feature Identity Model - Comprehensive Analysis

## Overview
The storage feature (`storage-local-baseline`) is a **fully autonomous, dependency-free** module. It does NOT import types from `frame` or `receive` - it has its own complete type system.

---

## 1. CORE TYPE DEFINITIONS

### 1.1 Primary Record Type: `StorageLocalRecord`

**Location:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/core/types.ts`

```typescript
export interface StorageLocalRecord {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly capturedAt: string;
  readonly source: StorageLocalRecordSource;
  readonly channel: string;
  readonly fields: readonly StorageRecordField[];
  readonly note?: string;
}

export type StorageLocalRecordSource = (typeof STORAGE_LOCAL_RECORD_SOURCES)[number];
export const STORAGE_LOCAL_RECORD_SOURCES = ['local', 'legacy', 'manual'] as const;
```

**Key Identity Fields:**
- `id`: **Storage-generated primary identifier** (must be non-empty string)
- `capturedAt`: ISO 8601 timestamp for when data was captured
- `source`: Origin of the record (`'local'` | `'legacy'` | `'manual'`)
- `channel**: Logical grouping identifier (e.g., 'telemetry-a', 'telemetry-b')
- `fields`: Array of key-value pairs with optional units
- `note`: Optional user annotation

---

### 1.2 Field Type: `StorageRecordField`

```typescript
export interface StorageRecordField {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly value: StorageRecordFieldValue;
  readonly unit?: string;
}

export type StorageRecordFieldValue = string | number | boolean | null;
```

**Field Identity:**
- `key`: Field identifier (e.g., 'temperature', 'status')
- `value`: Field value (limited to primitive types)
- `unit`: Optional measurement unit (e.g., 'C' for Celsius)

---

### 1.3 Material Types (Container Concepts)

#### `StorageHistoryMaterial` - Hour-grouped records
```typescript
export interface StorageHistoryMaterial {
  readonly hourKey: string;  // Format: "YYYY-MM-DDTHH" (e.g., "2026-04-30T08")
  readonly source: StorageLocalRecordSource;
  readonly records: readonly StorageLocalRecord[];
}
```

#### `StorageCsvMaterial` - CSV export artifact
```typescript
export interface StorageCsvMaterial {
  readonly id: string;              // CSV material ID (storage-generated)
  readonly name: string;            // Human-readable name
  readonly generatedAt: string;     // ISO 8601 timestamp
  readonly columns: readonly StorageCsvColumn[];
  readonly recordCount: number;
  readonly content: string;         // Actual CSV content
}

export interface StorageCsvColumn {
  readonly key: string;     // Field key to extract from records
  readonly label: string;   // Column header in CSV
}
```

#### `StorageLegacyJsonMaterial` - Legacy data wrapper
```typescript
export interface StorageLegacyJsonMaterial {
  readonly key: string;           // Legacy identifier (user-provided)
  readonly itemCount: number;     // Number of items in array
  readonly acceptedAt: string;    // ISO 8601 timestamp
}
```

---

### 1.4 Query & Summary Types

#### Query Interface
```typescript
export interface StorageRecordQuery {
  readonly from?: string;           // Start time (ISO 8601)
  readonly to?: string;             // End time (ISO 8601)
  readonly channel?: string;        // Filter by channel
  readonly fieldKeys?: readonly string[];  // Filter by field presence
}
```

#### Hour Summary
```typescript
export interface StorageHourSummary {
  readonly hourKey: string;
  readonly recordCount: number;
  readonly firstCapturedAt: string;
  readonly lastCapturedAt: string;
}
```

---

## 2. HOW STORAGE RECEIVES DATA

### 2.1 Data Input Points

**Storage does NOT receive data directly from `receive` or `frame` features.** Instead, it provides APIs for external systems to push data into it.

**Location:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/services/storage-local-service.ts`

```typescript
export interface StorageLocalService {
  // Load records from persistent storage (via adapter)
  loadLocalRecords(materialId: string): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
  
  // Append new records to existing collection
  appendLocalRecords(records: readonly StorageLocalRecord[]): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
  
  // Load historical materials
  loadHistoryMaterials(materialIds: readonly string[]): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
  
  // Create CSV from existing records
  createCsvFromLocalRecords(input: Omit<CreateStorageCsvMaterialInput, 'records'> & {
    readonly query?: StorageRecordQuery;
  }): Promise<StorageLocalCsvOperationResult>;
  
  // Save legacy JSON material
  saveLegacyJsonMaterial(key: string, value: unknown, acceptedAt: string): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
  
  // Copy entire state snapshot
  copySnapshot(snapshotId: string): Promise<StorageLocalOperationResult>;
}
```

---

### 2.2 Data Normalization Pipeline

**Location:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/core/validation.ts`

Storage validates and normalizes ALL incoming data:

```typescript
export function normalizeStorageLocalRecord(
  value: unknown,
  location = 'record',
): StorageLocalRecordNormalizeResult {
  // 1. Type checking: must be plain object
  // 2. Required fields: id, capturedAt, source, channel, fields
  // 3. Field validation within records
  // 4. Returns normalized record or validation issues
}
```

**Validation Rules:**
- `id`: Must be non-empty string (trimmed)
- `capturedAt`: Must be valid ISO 8601 timestamp (`Date.parse()` must return finite number)
- `source`: Must be one of `'local'`, `'legacy'`, `'manual'` (defaults to `'local'` if missing)
- `channel`: Must be non-empty string (trimmed)
- `fields`: Must be non-empty array
  - Each field: `key` (non-empty), `value` (primitive only), `unit` (optional string)
  - Field keys must be unique within a record
- `note`: Optional string (trimmed if present)

---

## 3. STORAGE'S IDENTIFIER CREATION

### 3.1 Record Identifiers

**Storage does NOT auto-generate record IDs.** Records must come with IDs already assigned.

```typescript
// From validation.ts line 129-132
const id = isText(value.id) ? value.id.trim() : '';
if (!id) {
  issues.push(createStorageIssue('record.idMissing', `${location}.id`, '本地记录 ID 不能为空'));
}
```

**Implication:** External systems (like `receive` feature) must generate record IDs before passing to storage.

---

### 3.2 Material Identifiers

Storage DOES generate IDs for materials:

#### CSV Material IDs
```typescript
// From csv.ts line 77
const id = input.id.trim();  // User provides the ID
if (!id) {
  issues.push(createStorageIssue('csv.idMissing', 'csv.id', 'CSV 材料 ID 不能为空'));
}
```

#### Legacy Material Keys
```typescript
// From legacy.ts line 23
const normalizedKey = key.trim();  // User provides the key
```

#### Hour Keys (Auto-generated)
```typescript
// From history.ts line 35-36
export function getStorageHourKey(capturedAt: string): string {
  return new Date(capturedAt).toISOString().slice(0, 13);  // "YYYY-MM-DDTHH"
}
```

---

### 3.3 Channel Identifiers

Storage uses channels as **logical groupings**, not physical connections:

```typescript
// From fixtures - example channel names
channel: 'telemetry-a'
channel: 'telemetry-b'
```

Channels are **user-defined strings** that serve as:
1. Filtering criteria in queries
2. Grouping mechanism for related data
3. Export organization (CSVs can be created per channel)

**No validation beyond non-empty string check** - storage accepts any channel name.

---

## 4. MAPPING: RECEIVE DATA → STORAGE RECORD

**Critical Finding:** Storage does NOT import from `receive`. However, we can infer the mapping by comparing type structures:

### 4.1 Hypothetical Mapping (if integrating with receive)

**Receive Feature Types** (from `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/receive/core/types.ts`):

```typescript
// Receive has these identifiers:
interface ReceiveParsedFieldValue {
  readonly frameId: string;        // Frame identifier
  readonly frameName: string;      // Frame name
  readonly fieldId: string;        // Field identifier
  readonly fieldName: string;      // Field name
  readonly dataType: FrameDataType;
  readonly offset: number;
  readonly length: number;
  readonly rawHex: string;
  readonly value: ReceiveParsedFieldPrimitive;  // number | string | null
  readonly displayValue: string;
  readonly label?: string;
}
```

**Mapping Logic:**

| Receive Field | Storage Field | Transformation |
|--------------|---------------|----------------|
| `frameId` + timestamp | `id` | Composite ID generation |
| `frameId`/`frameName` | `channel` | Use frame identifier as channel |
| Timestamp of receipt | `capturedAt` | Direct copy |
| `'local'` | `source` | Fixed value |
| `fieldName` | `fields[].key` | Direct copy |
| `value` or `displayValue` | `fields[].value` | Type coercion to primitives |
| `label` or unit metadata | `fields[].unit` | Optional mapping |

---

### 4.2 Channel Mapping Strategy

**Option 1: Frame-based channels**
```typescript
channel = frameName  // e.g., "telemetry-frame"
```

**Option 2: Source-based channels**
```typescript
channel = `${connectionId}-${frameName}`  // e.g., "serial1-telemetry"
```

**Option 3: Custom user-defined**
```typescript
channel = userProvidedChannel  // Configured per frame
```

---

## 5. STORAGE-SPECIFIC IDENTIFIERS

### 5.1 Not Present in Source Data

These identifiers are **created by storage internally**:

1. **Hour Keys** (`StorageHistoryMaterial.hourKey`)
   - Generated from `capturedAt` timestamps
   - Format: `"YYYY-MM-DDTHH"`
   - Used for grouping records into hourly materials

2. **CSV Material IDs** (`StorageCsvMaterial.id`)
   - User-provided at export time
   - Must be unique within CSV bucket
   - No auto-generation (validation error if missing)

3. **Material Bucket IDs** (`StorageMaterialBucket`)
   - Fixed enumeration: `'records'`, `'history'`, `'csv'`, `'legacy'`, `'snapshot'`
   - Used by adapter for organizing persistent storage

---

### 5.2 Persistent Storage Organization

**Location:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/adapters/ports.ts`

```typescript
export interface LocalMaterialAdapter {
  readMaterial(bucket: StorageMaterialBucket, id: string): Promise<LocalMaterialReadResult>;
  writeMaterial(bucket: StorageMaterialBucket, id: string, value: unknown): Promise<LocalMaterialWriteResult>;
  deleteMaterial(bucket: StorageMaterialBucket, id: string): Promise<LocalMaterialDeleteResult>;
  listMaterials(bucket: StorageMaterialBucket): Promise<LocalMaterialListResult>;
}
```

**Storage Organization:**
```
records/          ← Raw record collections
  ├── local-records
  ├── user-defined-1
  └── ...
history/          ← Hourly grouped materials
  ├── 2026-04-30T08
  ├── 2026-04-30T09
  └── ...
csv/              ← Export artifacts
  ├── export-001
  ├── telemetry-april
  └── ...
legacy/           ← Legacy JSON imports
  ├── old-config
  ├── archived-data
  └── ...
snapshot/         ← Full state backups
  ├── backup-001
  └── ...
```

---

## 6. DEPENDENCY CHAIN ANALYSIS

### 6.1 Storage's Dependencies

**Storage imports ONLY from:**
1. `@/shared/types/readonly-deep` - Shared utility type
2. Internal core modules

**NO imports from:**
- `@/features/frame`
- `@/features/receive`
- `@/features/send`
- Any other feature

### 6.2 Feature Dependency Graph

```
┌─────────────┐
│   shared    │  ← ReadonlyDeep type
└──────┬──────┘
       │
       ▼
┌─────────────────────────┐
│ storage-local-baseline  │  ← Autonomous feature
└─────────────────────────┘
       ▲
       │ (uses, but doesn't import from)
       │
┌──────┴──────┐
│   receive   │  ← Would map ReceiveFieldValue → StorageLocalRecord
│    frame    │  ← Provides frame definitions for mapping
└─────────────┘
```

---

## 7. VALIDATION & ERROR HANDLING

### 7.1 Validation Result Type

```typescript
export interface StorageValidationResult {
  readonly valid: boolean;
  readonly issues: readonly StorageValidationIssue[];
}

export interface StorageValidationIssue {
  readonly severity: StorageValidationSeverity;  // 'error' | 'warning'
  readonly code: string;                         // Machine-readable code
  readonly location: string;                     // Path to issue
  readonly message: string;                      // Human-readable message
}
```

### 7.2 Error Codes

**Record-level errors:**
- `record.invalid` - Not a plain object
- `record.idMissing` - Empty or missing ID
- `record.capturedAtInvalid` - Invalid timestamp
- `record.sourceUnsupported` - Invalid source value
- `record.channelMissing` - Empty channel
- `record.fieldsMissing` - No fields array
- `record.fieldKeyDuplicate` - Duplicate field keys

**Material-level errors:**
- `csv.idMissing` - Missing CSV material ID
- `csv.recordsEmpty` - No records to export
- `history.hourKeyInvalid` - Invalid hour key format
- `legacy.keyMissing` - Missing legacy key

---

## 8. KEY INSIGHTS

### 8.1 Identity Model Summary

| Identifier | Source | Format | Auto-generated? |
|------------|--------|--------|-----------------|
| `record.id` | External | String (user-defined) | **No** |
| `record.channel` | External | String (user-defined) | **No** |
| `history.hourKey` | Storage | `"YYYY-MM-DDTHH"` | **Yes** |
| `csv.id` | User | String (user-provided) | **No** |
| `legacy.key` | User | String (user-provided) | **No** |

### 8.2 Data Flow Summary

```
External System
    │
    │ (provides records with pre-generated IDs)
    ▼
Storage Service
    │
    ├─→ Normalization (validation)
    ├─→ Merging (deduplication by ID)
    ├─→ Querying (filter by channel/time/fields)
    └─→ Export (CSV, history materials)
```

### 8.3 Design Principles

1. **Autonomy:** Storage has no dependencies on other features
2. **Validation-first:** All incoming data is normalized and validated
3. **User-controlled IDs:** External systems control record identification
4. **Flexible channels:** Channels are generic strings, no validation
5. **Immutable snapshots:** State is managed via snapshots, not mutations
6. **Bucket-based persistence:** Clear separation of concerns via buckets

---

## 9. PUBLIC API SUMMARY

**Location:** `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/index.ts`

```typescript

  StorageLocalRecord,

  summarizeStorageHours,
  cleanupStorageLocalRecords,
  createStorageCsvMaterial,
  getStorageHourKey,
};

// Service creation
export { createStorageLocalService, createStorageLocalReader };
```

---

This analysis shows that storage is a **pure data persistence layer** with its own complete identity model, designed to accept records from any external system that can provide properly formatted data with pre-assigned IDs.

## Cross-Feature Identity Mapping Audit

### Mapping Analysis Table

| Location | Input → Output | Complexity (1-5) | Information Loss | Justified | Notes |
|----------|---------------|------------------|------------------|-----------|-------|
| **connection-to-receive.ts:14-30** | `TransportEventSnapshot.connectionId` → `ReceiveInputEvent.source.sourceId` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **Yes** | Same identifier, different property name for source abstraction |
| **connection-to-receive.ts:14-30** | `TransportEventSnapshot.target.targetId` → `ReceiveInputEvent.source.targetId` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **Yes** | Direct pass-through of optional target identifier |
| **receive-display-bridge.ts:16** | `ReceiveParsedFieldValue.frameId` → `DisplayFieldMaterial.groupId` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **No** | Simple rename - same semantic meaning |
| **receive-display-bridge.ts:17** | `ReceiveParsedFieldValue.fieldId` → `DisplayFieldMaterial.dataItemId` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **No** | Simple rename - same semantic meaning |
| **receive-storage-bridge.ts:13** | `ReceiveInputBatch.source.sourceId` → `StorageLocalRecord.channel` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **Partial** | Domain-appropriate rename (source→channel), but same value |
| **receive-storage-bridge.ts:15** | `ReceiveParsedFieldValue.fieldName` → `StorageRecordField.key` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **Yes** | Domain-appropriate rename for key-value storage |
| **routing-tick.ts:99-100** | `ReceiveBatchOutcome.frameId/fieldId` → `ConditionMatchInput.frameId/fieldId` | **1** (trivial) | **No** | **Yes** | Direct pass-through for task system |

### Key Findings

**1. Connection → Receive Bridge** (`connection-to-receive.ts:14-30`)
- **Purpose**: Adapts transport-layer events to receive input format
- **Complexity**: Trivial - direct field assignments with optional chaining
- **Information Loss**: None - all identifiers preserved
- **Justification**: **Justified** - creates source abstraction layer, separates transport concerns from receive logic

**2. Receive → Display Bridge** (`receive-display-bridge.ts:16-17`)
- **Purpose**: Routes parsed field values to display system
- **Complexity**: Trivial - simple property renames
- **Information Loss**: None - direct 1:1 mapping
- **Justification**: **Not Justified** - `frameId→groupId` and `fieldId→dataItemId` are pure renames with no semantic difference. The display feature doesn't use "group" or "dataItem" concepts elsewhere - it's just inconsistent terminology for the same concepts.

**3. Receive → Storage Bridge** (`receive-storage-bridge.ts:13,15`)
- **Purpose**: Persists received data to local storage
- **Complexity**: Trivial - direct field assignments
- **Information Loss**: None - all information preserved
- **Justification**: **Partially Justified** - `fieldName→key` rename makes sense for key-value storage semantics. `sourceId→channel` is domain-appropriate but creates unnecessary mapping complexity.

**4. Receive → Task Routing** (`routing-tick.ts:99-100`)
- **Purpose**: Feeds matched field values to task condition evaluation
- **Complexity**: Trivial - direct pass-through
- **Information Loss**: None
- **Justification**: **Justified** - task system needs the same identifiers as receive for frame/field targeting

### Recommendations

1. **Display Feature Renaming is Unjustified**: The `frameId→groupId` and `fieldId→dataItemId` mappings provide no semantic benefit. Consider standardizing Display to use `frameId`/`fieldId` directly, eliminating the bridge entirely.

2. **Storage Renaming is Acceptable**: The `fieldName→key` and `sourceId→channel` renames reflect appropriate domain language for storage systems.

3. **All Mappings Are Reversible**: No information is lost in any bridge, which is good for debugging and traceability.

4. **Low Complexity Overhead**: While trivial individually, the cumulative mapping layer adds unnecessary cognitive load when reasoning about data flow across features.

### Critical Code Evidence

**Unnecessary Display Mapping** (`receive-display-bridge.ts:15-22`):
```typescript
fields.push({
  groupId: f.frameId,        // Line 16: Pure rename, no semantic change
  dataItemId: f.fieldId,     // Line 17: Pure rename, no semantic change
  fieldName: f.fieldName,
  value: f.value,
  displayValue: f.displayValue,
  updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,
});
```

**Storage Domain Adaptation** (`receive-storage-bridge.ts:9-18`):
```typescript
return {
  // ...
  channel: outcome.input.source.sourceId,  // Line 13: Domain-appropriate rename
  fields: outcome.fields.map((f) => ({
    key: f.fieldName,  // Line 15: Semantic adaptation to key-value storage
    value: f.value ?? null,
  })),
};
```

The Display feature's identifier renaming appears to be historical artifact rather than intentional design, creating unnecessary mapping overhead without providing any domain-specific semantic value.

Based on my comprehensive search of the dongfanghong rewrite project, here is a detailed report on existing identity-related utility functions and patterns:

## Identity-Related Utilities and Patterns Found

### 1. **Compound Key Pattern: `fieldKey()` Function**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/receive/state/receive-state.ts` (lines 120-122)

```typescript
function fieldKey(field: Pick<ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot, 'frameId' | 'fieldId'>): string {
  return `${field.frameId}:${field.fieldId}`;
}
```

**Usage**: Used in the same file for creating a Map key to merge field values:
```typescript
const merged = new Map(current.map((item) => [fieldKey(item), cloneFieldValue(item)]));
for (const field of outcome.fields) {
  merged.set(fieldKey(field), { ...field, updatedAt: outcome.processedAt, sourceId: outcome.input.source.sourceId });
}
```

### 2. **Display Feature: Compound Key Construction**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/core/projection.ts` (line 57)

```typescript
const field = fields.find((f) => `${f.groupId}:${f.dataItemId}` === fieldId);
```

**Pattern**: Inline compound key construction using template literals with colon (`:`) separator.

### 3. **Connection Feature: Compound Key Generation**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/lifecycle.ts`

Multiple examples of compound key generation:
- Line 62: `${config.host}:${config.port}`
- Line 65: `${config.remoteHost}:${config.remotePort}`
- Line 66: `${config.localHost}:${config.localPort}`
- Line 76: `targetId: `transport:${config.id}``
- Line 118: `id: `${event.connectionId}:${event.kind}:${event.occurredAt}``

### 4. **Storage Feature: Hour-based Key Generation**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/core/history.ts` (lines 35-37)

```typescript
export function getStorageHourKey(capturedAt: string): string {
  return new Date(capturedAt).toISOString().slice(0, 13);
}
```

### 5. **Identity Patterns Across Features**

#### **Receive Feature** (frameId:fieldId pattern)
- **Type**: `ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot` uses `frameId` and `fieldId`
- **Index Pattern**: `ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>>` - nested Map structure
- **Usage**: Building read model index in `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/receive/state/receive-state.ts` (lines 213-223)

#### **Display Feature** (groupId:dataItemId pattern)
- **Types**: 
  - `DisplayFieldMaterial` (lines 48-55 in types.ts)
  - `ScatterSourceBinding` (lines 25-28 in types.ts)
  - `TableRowProjection` (lines 69-76 in types.ts)
- **Pattern Consistency**: Uses `groupId` and `dataItemId` throughout

#### **Status Feature** (groupId:dataItemId pattern)
- **Types**: 
  - `IndicatorConfig` (lines 46-56 in status/core/types.ts)
  - `IndicatorProjection` (lines 72-80 in status/core/types.ts)
  - `ReceiveFieldMaterial` (lines 25-30 in status/core/types.ts)

### 6. **Runtime Bridge Pattern**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/bridges/receive-display-bridge.ts`

Shows the transformation from `frameId:fieldId` (receive) to `groupId:dataItemId` (display):
```typescript
fields.push({
  groupId: f.frameId,      // frameId becomes groupId
  dataItemId: f.fieldId,   // fieldId becomes dataItemId
  fieldName: f.fieldName,
  value: f.value,
  displayValue: f.displayValue,
  updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,
});
```

### 7. **Shared Utilities Available**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/shared/utils/`

- **clone-unknown.ts**: Generic deep cloning utility
- **timestamp.ts**: Simple timestamp generation (`defaultNow()`)
- **No shared key/ID utilities found**

### 8. **Timer Registry Group ID Pattern**
**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/shared/timer/timer-registry.ts`

Uses `groupId` for grouping timers (different from data identity):
```typescript
register(id: string, callback: () => void, intervalMs: number, groupId = 'default'): string
```

## Key Findings Summary

1. **No centralized `fieldKey()` or `compoundKey()` utility exists** in shared utilities
2. **Colon (`:`) separator is the standard pattern** for compound keys across the codebase
3. **Two main compound key patterns**:
   - `frameId:fieldId` (receive feature)
   - `groupId:dataItemId` (display/status features)
4. **Map-based indexing is common** for efficient lookups
5. **Inline template literal construction** is the typical approach (no utility functions)
6. **ID transformation occurs at runtime bridges** between features

## Reusable Patterns

1. **Compound key format**: `${parentKey}:${childKey}` (e.g., `frameId:fieldId`)
2. **Nested Map structure**: `Map<string, Map<string, ValueType>>` for hierarchical data
3. **Key generation**: Template literals with colon separator
4. **Normalization**: Features transform IDs at bridge boundaries

The codebase would benefit from a shared compound key utility, but currently relies on inline implementations following consistent patterns.

我现在有所有证据来提供一个完整的分析。让我来综合我的发现。

## 总结

我分析了 display、storage-local-baseline、receive 和 frame 这四个功能的身份模型，以及连接它们的两个桥接文件。当前的映射已经是微不足道的1:1，只是使用了不同的字段名。这产生了一个清晰的建议：方案 C（简化现状）是务实的选择，方案 A（统一身份）是增量改进最多但前期成本最高的方案。方案 B 引入了不必要的复杂性。

## 分析

### 证据：实际的身份流动

**接收 -> 显示桥接** (`receive-display-bridge.ts:14-18`):
```ts
groupId: f.frameId,
dataItemId: f.fieldId,
```
这是一个字面的1:1重命名。`groupId` = `frameId`，`dataItemId` = `fieldId`。没有转换，没有聚合，没有合成逻辑。

**接收 -> 存储桥接** (`receive-storage-bridge.ts:13-15`):
```ts
channel: outcome.input.source.sourceId,
key: f.fieldName,
```
这很**不**是一个1:1重命名。`channel` 来自 `source.sourceId`（连接目标，而不是帧），而 `key` 来自 `fieldName`（而不是 `fieldId`）。存储使用 `sourceId` 作为通道，使用人类可读的名称作为字段键。这意味着存储的标识模型与帧/接收身份模型根本不同——它按连接源分组，而不是按帧定义分组。

**显示复合键** (`projection.ts:57`):
```ts
const field = fields.find((f) => `${f.groupId}:${f.dataItemId}` === fieldId);
```
图表历史缓冲区使用`"groupId:dataItemId"`作为复合键来存储历史点。这个字符串只有当它 elsewhere 等于`"frameId:fieldId"`时才起作用。该约定是隐式的，而不是强制执行的。

**散点源绑定** (`types.ts:26-28`):
```ts
interface ScatterSourceBinding {
  readonly groupId: string;
  readonly dataItemId: string;
}
```
I/Q 散点图选择器单独存储 `groupId` 和 `dataItemId`，然后在 `projection.ts:102-103` 中匹配：
```ts
fields.find((f) => f.groupId === binding.groupId && f.dataItemId === binding.dataItemId)
```

**表格偏好设置** (`types.ts:12-13`):
```ts
interface TableDisplayPreference {
  readonly selectedGroupId: string;
  readonly selectedItems: readonly string[];
}
```
表格选择器使用 `selectedGroupId` 进行过滤，使用 `selectedItems` 进行特定数据项过滤。在灯具 `display-fixtures.ts:62` 中，图表的 `selectedItems` 包含复合键 `['g1:f1', 'g1:f2']`。

**存储独立性**：`StorageLocalRecord` (`storage-local-baseline/core/types.ts:15-22`) 使用 `channel` + `StorageRecordField.key`。在桥接中，`channel` 映射到 `sourceId`（而不是帧），而 `key` 映射到 `fieldName`（而不是 `fieldId`）。存储的标识模型用于：“哪些连接源生成了这些数据，以及字段的名称是什么？”它与帧/接收的标识模型正交。

### 关键见解：桥接不对称

接收->显示桥接是一个纯粹的标识重命名（`frameId`/`fieldId` -> `groupId`/`dataItemId`）。但接收->存储桥接则根本不同——它按传输源分组并使用人类可读的字段名称。这意味着：

1.  **显示**已经使用帧身份，只是用了不同的名称。统一处理几乎是零成本的。
2.  **存储**有意识地使用不同的身份模型（按源通道，而非按帧定义）。统一处理将需要架构重新设计或一个奇怪的“`frameId` 作为通道”的技巧，这会丢失语义。

---

## 方案 A：统一身份（`frameId`/`fieldId` 无处不在）

### 每个功能的标识字段

| 功能 | 标识字段 | 变化 |
|---------|---------------|--------|
| **帧** | `FrameAsset.id`, `FrameFieldDefinition.id` | 无变化（事实来源） |
| **接收** | `frameId`, `fieldId` | 无变化（已使用帧身份） |
| **显示** | `frameId`, `fieldId`（取代 `groupId`/`dataItemId`） | 重命名字段 |
| **存储** | `channel` = `frameId`，`key` = `fieldId` | **重大变化**：从 `sourceId`/`fieldName` 语义 |

### 桥接变化

**接收->显示桥接** (`receive-display-bridge.ts`):
```ts
// Before:
groupId: f.frameId,
dataItemId: f.fieldId,
// After: pass through directly, no rename
frameId: f.frameId,
fieldId: f.fieldId,
```
桥接变得几乎冗余——身份字段直接透传。

**接收->存储桥接** (`receive-storage-bridge.ts`):
```ts
// Before:
channel: outcome.input.source.sourceId,  // connection source
key: f.fieldName,                         // human-readable name
// After:
channel: f.frameId,    // now frame-based
key: f.fieldId,        // now field ID
```
这**破坏**了存储的“按通道分组”语义。目前，`channel` 意味着“哪个连接产生了这些数据”（例如 `'serial-1'`、`'tcp-conn-3'`）。在方案 A 下，`channel` 变为“哪个帧定义匹配了这些数据”（例如 `'frame-voltage-sensor'`）。信息丢失：您无法再区分来自同一帧类型不同连接的记录。

### 破坏性变更

1.  **显示类型**：`DisplayFieldMaterial`、`TableDisplayPreference`、`ScatterSourceBinding`、`TableRowProjection`、`ChartSeriesProjection`——所有使用 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的类型都必须重命名字段。大约有 **8 个文件** 包含引用这些的类型或逻辑。
2.  **显示投影逻辑**：`projection.ts:57` 中的复合键 `"groupId:dataItemId"` 变为 `"frameId:fieldId"`。如果 `selectedItems` 中的任何偏好数据被持久化（它们通过设置被持久化），序列化的格式将会改变。
3.  **显示灯具**：所有灯具都使用了 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的符号名称（`display-fixtures.ts` 中有 4+ 个灯具）。
4.  **显示规格**：所有测试引用了 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的字段选择器。
5.  **存储语义**：`channel` 丢失了其“连接源”的含义。历史查询和 CSV 导出，如果它们按通道分组/显示，将会显示帧名称而不是源标识符。
6.  **设置持久化**：如果任何偏好快照被序列化（表选择的 `selectedGroupId`、散点 `iSource`/`qSource` 绑定），持久化的格式将不兼容。

### 新增约束

-   存储必须添加一个单独的 `sourceId` 字段以保留连接来源，或者存储必须接受来自同一 `channel`（帧）的记录可能源自不同的连接。
-   存储字段 `key` 必须全局唯一（因为 `fieldId` 是每个帧的，所以不同帧中的两个 `fieldId = 'field1'` 的字段会冲突）。需要复合键：`frameId + fieldId` 或类似方案。
-   偏好序列化格式必须从 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 迁移到 `frameId`/`fieldId`。

### 对已锁定设计的影响

-   **显示设计文档**：符号字段名 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 在显示设计文档的类型定义中被引用。该设计将需要勘误或版本升级。
-   **存储设计文档**：`channel` 语义从“连接源标识符”变为“帧标识符”。存储设计文档的第 4.4 节明确指出，存储不定义接收解析真相。将 `frameId` 作为通道模糊了这一边界——存储现在承载了帧身份，这是一个领域引用。

### 工作量估计：**12-15 个文件**（显示：约 8 个文件，存储：约 4 个文件，桥接：约 2 个文件，测试：约 4 个文件）

---

## 方案 B：双重身份（定义 ID + 运行时 ID）

### 每个功能的标识字段

| 功能 | 定义 ID（模板） | 运行时 ID（操作型） |
|---------|---------------------|--------------------------|
| **帧** | `frameId`, `fieldId` | -- |
| **接收** | `frameId`, `fieldId` | `outcome.id`（批次 ID），`source.sourceId` |
| **显示** | `frameId`, `fieldId`（用于偏好/绑定） | 操作型透传 |
| **存储** | `frameId`, `fieldId`（用于帧引用） | `channel` = `sourceId`，记录 `id` |

### 设计

每个功能都携带：
-   一个**定义层**身份，用于引用帧模板（用于“这个显示项是哪个帧字段？”）。
-   一个**运行时层**身份，用于操作型数据（用于“这个值是什么时候捕获的，来自哪里？”）。

显示将使用 `frameId`/`fieldId` 来实现 `ScatterSourceBinding` 和 `TableDisplayPreference`，并丢弃 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的重命名。存储将添加 `frameId` + `fieldId` 作为 `StorageRecordField` 上的可选引用字段，与现有 `channel` + `key` 分开。

### 桥接变化

**接收->显示桥接**：变为透传（同方案 A）。

**接收->存储桥接**：
```ts
return {
  id: outcome.id,
  channel: outcome.input.source.sourceId,   // preserved: connection source
  fields: outcome.fields.map((f) => ({
    key: f.fieldName,                        // preserved: human-readable
    value: f.value ?? null,
    frameId: f.frameId,                      // NEW: definition reference
    fieldId: f.fieldId,                      // NEW: definition reference
  })),
};
```

### 破坏性变更

1.  显示类型重命名：同方案 A（`groupId`/`dataItemId` -> `frameId`/`fieldId`）。
2.  `StorageRecordField` 类型获得两个新的可选字段（`frameId?`、`fieldId?`）。这是一个**添加性**更改——现有代码不会中断。
3.  存储灯具和规格需要更新以包含新的可选字段或明确省略它们。

### 新增约束

-   存储必须定义当 `frameId`/`fieldId` 不可用时的行为（旧数据、手动记录、非帧来源）。
-   显示偏好序列化格式迁移（同方案 A）。
-   需要一个清晰的规则：“定义字段何时是可选的，何时是必需的？”存储中的手动/遗留记录不会有它们。

### 对已锁定设计的影响

-   **显示设计**：同方案 A——符号重命名。
-   **存储设计**：影响小于方案 A。`channel` 保持其连接源语义。新的定义引用是添加性的。但存储设计第 4.4 节指出，存储不定义帧语义。添加 `frameId`/`fieldId` 创建了一个反向引用，必须被记录为“弱引用，而非所有权”。

### 工作量估计：**10-13 个文件**（显示：约 8 个文件，存储类型+桥接：约 3 个文件，测试：约 4 个文件）

### 对方案 B 的批评

此方案将存储记录变成了一个既承载身份模型又承载运行时身份的“混合体”。实际上，双重身份已经存在：

-   存储已经拥有 `channel`（运行时：连接源）和 `key`（显示：字段名）。
-   接收已经拥有 `frameId`/`fieldId`（定义）和 `outcome.id`/`sourceId`（运行时）。
-   显示已经拥有 `groupId`/`dataItemId`（定义代理）和 `updatedAt`（运行时间戳）。

方案 B 正式化了已经非正式存在的东西，但代价是增加了存储字段上的字段膨胀。问题是：**谁真正需要存储中的定义身份？**只有未来的功能（如报告、历史图表）需要在历史数据中查找“哪个帧字段是这个？”对于当前的 MVP，存储按通道查询并按 `key` 显示字段就足够了。添加 `frameId`/`fieldId` 是对未来需求的过早优化。

---

## 方案 C：简化现状（保留不同名称，显式化映射）

### 每个功能的标识字段

| 功能 | 标识字段 | 变化 |
|---------|---------------|--------|
| **帧** | `FrameAsset.id`, `FrameFieldDefinition.id` | 无变化 |
| **接收** | `frameId`, `fieldId` | 无变化 |
| **显示** | `frameId`, `fieldId`（重命名自 `groupId`/`dataItemId`） | **仅限重命名** |
| **存储** | `channel`（源），`key`（字段名） | 无变化 |

### 设计

该方案认识到重命名有两种类型：

1.  **显示重命名**（`frameId`->`groupId`，`fieldId`->`dataItemId`）纯粹是装饰性的。它没有增加语义价值。代码库已经有 `f.frameId` 映射到 `groupId`。只需使用 `frameId`/`fieldId` 并删除重命名。

2.  **存储映射**（`sourceId`->`channel`，`fieldName`->`key`）是**有语义的**。它将身份从“帧定义”转换为“运行时源+人类可读名称”。这种映射应该被保留和显式化。

具体更改：

**显示类型**：将所有 `groupId` 重命名为 `frameId`，将所有 `dataItemId` 重命名为 `fieldId`。这将使 `DisplayFieldMaterial` 与 `ReceiveParsedFieldValue` 保持一致。

**显示投影**：`projection.ts:57` 中的复合键变为 `${f.frameId}:${f.fieldId}`，它现在明确地是一个帧引用，而不是一个不透明的“组:项”约定。

**散点绑定**：`ScatterSourceBinding` 变为 `{ frameId: string, fieldId: string }`，这向读者明确表示绑定指向一个帧定义。

**存储**：根本不改变。`channel` = 连接源，`key` = 字段名称的语义在本地存储上下文中是正确且有价值的。

### 桥接变化

**接收->显示桥接** (`receive-display-bridge.ts`):
```ts
// Before:
groupId: f.frameId,
dataItemId: f.fieldId,
// After: identity fields pass through, only display-specific enrichment remains
frameId: f.frameId,
fieldId: f.fieldId,
```
桥接仍然存在，因为它执行过滤（跳过不匹配的、空字段）并构造 `DisplayFieldMaterial` 对象。但身份映射是透明的。

**接收->存储桥接**：不改变。映射是有意的并且已被保留。

### 破坏性变更

1.  **显示类型重命名**：`DisplayFieldMaterial`、`TableDisplayPreference`、`ScatterSourceBinding`、`TableRowProjection` 会丢弃 `groupId`/`dataItemId`，改用 `frameId`/`fieldId`。大约有 **8 个显示文件**。
2.  **显示灯具**：所有灯具都将 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的符号名称更新为 `frameId`/`fieldId`。
3.  **显示规格**：所有引用这些字段名的测试断言。
4.  **偏好序列化**：`selectedGroupId` 变为 `selectedFrameId`。任何持久化的设置数据都包含旧字段名。需要一个迁移工具或版本升级。

### 新增约束

-   显式规则：显示偏好使用 `frameId`/`fieldId` 直接引用帧定义。不存在任何间接性。
-   显式规则：存储使用 `channel`（连接源）和 `key`（显示标签）。未来需要帧定义引用的功能（报告、历史图表）必须查询帧功能或将 `frameId`/`fieldId` 携带为单独的字段。
-   偏好版本升级：如果任何持久化的设置包含 `selectedGroupId`/`dataItemId`/`groupId` 键，则需要进行迁移。

### 对已锁定设计的影响

-   **显示设计文档**：包含 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的类型定义需要勘误。语义保持不变；只是名称被统一。这是一个低风险的文档更改。
-   **存储设计文档**：无影响。存储的身份模型未被触及。

### 工作量估计：**8-10 个文件**（显示核心/服务/灯具/规格：约 8 个文件，桥接：1 个文件，设置迁移：1 个文件）

---

## 权衡

| 选项 | 优势 | 劣势 | 努力程度 |
|--------|------|------|--------|
| **A：统一** | 单一身份词汇无处不在。没有映射函数。新开发者学习一种命名约定。 | 破坏存储的“按源通道分组”语义。存储字段键需要复合帧+字段。如果处理不当，高重新设计风险。 | 12-15 个文件 |
| **B：双重** | 显示获得清晰的帧身份。存储保留运行时身份并添加定义引用。未来功能（报告）可以交叉引用。 | 为存储添加 `frameId`/`fieldId` 作为可选字段——是过早优化吗？规则更复杂（何时是定义可选的？）。概念模型最复杂。 | 10-13 个文件 |
| **C：简化** | 只解决实际问题（显示重命名），同时保留有意的存储语义。概念模型最简单。最少量的改动。偏好评级升级仍然是必需的，但仅限于显示端。 | 存储仍然与帧身份断开。未来需要“哪个帧是这个历史字段？”的功能将需要单独的映射或桥梁扩展。 | 8-10 个文件 |

## 建议

**方案 C 是务实的选择。**原因如下：

1.  显示重命名（`groupId`/`dataItemId` -> `frameId`/`fieldId`）已经是桥接中的字面量 1:1 映射。不同的名称没有语义上的理由——这是一个偶然的设计选择，应该予以修正。

2.  存储的身份模型（`channel` = 连接源，`key` = 字段显示名称）服务于不同的目的。它回答了“这个记录来自哪里，字段是什么？”而不是“哪个帧定义匹配了这个？”当存储需要帧定义引用时，这应该作为显式的添加（方案 B 的一种形式）在那时添加，而不是现在基于推测添加。

3.  方案 A 的成本（破坏存储通道语义、复合存储键）相对于收益（一种命名约定）来说过高。

4.  如果未来报告/历史功能需要，方案 B 是一个合理的**第二阶段扩展**。但它是一个添加性更改，不应该是第一阶段的决策。

Based on my comprehensive code analysis, here's the reverse verification report for each scenario:

## Reverse Verification Report: Scheme C (Identity Rename Only)

### **Scenario 1: Same parameter in table, chart, and storage**
**Verdict: VIABLE** ✓

**Evidence:**
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/core/projection.ts:57` - Chart projection uses compound key format: `` `${f.groupId}:${f.dataItemId}` === fieldId ``
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/bridges/receive-display-bridge.ts:16-17` - Already uses `frameId` → `groupId` mapping: `groupId: f.frameId, dataItemId: f.fieldId`
- After renaming to `frameId`/`fieldId`, the compound key becomes `"frameId:fieldId"` which maintains the same correlation mechanism

**No risks identified** - The rename is purely cosmetic and maintains the exact same compound key structure.

---

### **Scenario 2: Frame deletion with existing stored data**
**Verdict: VIABLE** ✓

**Evidence:**
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/services/display-service.ts:145-169` - Display service has no defensive handling for missing frames
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline/core/types.ts:19` - Storage uses `channel: string` independent of frame definitions
- The frame deletion scenario is **unchanged by the rename** - it behaves identically with `groupId`/`dataItemId` vs `frameId`/`fieldId`

**No additional risks** - The rename doesn't affect the frame deletion behavior, which was already undefined.

---

### **Scenario 3: Multiple connections (multi-source scenario)**
**Verdict: AT-RISK** ⚠️

**Evidence:**
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/bridges/receive-display-bridge.ts:14-23` - Multiple sources create identical `DisplayFieldMaterial` with same `frameId`/`fieldId` but different values
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/services/display-service.ts:149` - Buffer simply overwrites: `buffer.sourceFields = material.fields.map((f) => ({ ...f }))`
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/bridges/receive-storage-bridge.ts:13` - Storage separates by `channel: outcome.input.source.sourceId`

**Risks:**
1. **No multi-source deduplication** - Last source wins in display buffer
2. **Display-storage correlation breaks** - Display shows last received value, but storage stores all values separately by channel
3. **Cannot distinguish which source a displayed value came from**

**Mitigation needed:**
- Add `sourceId` to `DisplayFieldMaterial` 
- Include source context in display projections
- OR implement source priority/conflict resolution

---

### **Scenario 4: User preferences (favorites, filters, bindings)**
**Verdict: AT-RISK** ⚠️

**Evidence:**
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/core/types.ts:11-12,21,26-27` - Preferences use `selectedGroupId`, `selectedItems` format `"groupId:dataItemId"`, and `ScatterSourceBinding` with `groupId`/`dataItemId`
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/display/core/defaults.ts:9-14` - Default values use `groupId`/`dataItemId`
- **No persistence mechanism found** - No localStorage/sessionStorage usage detected

**Risks:**
1. **Schema version handling exists** (`DISPLAY_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`) but no migration logic for preference format changes
2. **Hardcoded test fixtures** use `groupId`/`dataItemId` format (`display-fixtures.ts:61` - `'g1:f1'`)
3. **Breaking change for any external consumers** of display preferences

**Mitigation needed:**
- Increment `DISPLAY_SCHEMA_VERSION` to `2`
- Add migration logic in normalization layer
- Update all test fixtures and examples

---

### **Scenario 5: Status feature uses similar pattern**
**Verdict: NOT VIABLE** ✗

**Evidence:**
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/status/core/types.ts:26-27,49-50` - Status feature uses **identical pattern**: `groupId`/`dataItemId` in `ReceiveFieldMaterial` and `IndicatorConfig`
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/status/core/indicator.ts:44,56` - Status indicators reference fields by `groupId`/`dataItemId`
- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/status/core/types.ts:75-76` - Projections also use `groupId`/`dataItemId`

**Risks:**
1. **Inconsistent terminology** across features if only display is renamed
2. **Receive feature acts as bridge** - uses `frameId`/`fieldId` internally but converts to `groupId`/`dataItemId` for display/status
3. **Coupled features** - Status and Display both consume from Receive using same naming

**Mitigation needed:**
- **Coordinated rename required** across Display, Status, and Receive features
- OR accept terminology inconsistency as acceptable trade-off

---

## Summary

| Scenario | Verdict | Risk Level | Mitigation Required |
|----------|---------|------------|-------------------|
| 1: Field correlation | VIABLE | None | None |
| 2: Frame deletion | VIABLE | None | None (unchanged behavior) |
| 3: Multi-source | AT-RISK | High | Add source awareness to display |
| 4: User preferences | AT-RISK | Medium | Schema version + migration |
| 5: Status feature | NOT VIABLE | Critical | Coordinated rename or accept inconsistency |

**Critical Issue:** The rename cannot be done in isolation. Either:
1. **Coordinated rename** across Display + Status features (recommended)
2. **Accept terminology inconsistency** between Display (frameId/fieldId) and Status (groupId/dataItemId)

**Recommended Approach:** Rename across ALL features (Display, Status, Receive) for consistency, addressing Scenarios 3-5 together with proper migration strategy.

**Evidence**:
1. Display feature **does not directly write** to localStorage/sessionStorage
2. Display preferences will be persisted **through settings feature** (see checklist DISP-UI-PRE-004)
3. Settings schema for display is **not yet implemented** (checklist shows pending)
4. No existing user data to migrate

**Design Doc Confirmation** (check DISP-UI-PRE-004):
```
"settings 只持久化事实，不解释 display 语义"
"display 偏好通过 settings public API 读写，display 不直接操作 localStorage"
```

**Conclusion**: Settings will store whatever field names display provides. Renaming before schema freeze = **zero migration cost**.

---

### Current Implementation Status

**Display Feature** (from checklist analysis):
- **Core foundation**: ✅ COMPLETE (types, normalization, projection, defaults)
- **Service/state/selector**: ✅ COMPLETE
- **UI implementation**: ❌ NOT STARTED (all DISP-UI-* items pending)
- **Settings integration**: ❌ NOT STARTED (DISP-UI-PRE-004 pending)
- **Storage conversion**: ❌ NOT STARTED (DISP-UI-PRE-005 pending)

**Total Lines of Display Code**: 1,384 (including tests/fixtures)

**Core Types/Logic**: 693 lines across 7 files

### Timing Recommendation: **NOW IS OPTIMAL** ⭐

**Rationale**:

1. **Zero UI Debt**: No Vue components, no templates, no user-facing strings to update
2. **Zero Runtime Integration**: Settings/schema not frozen, can accept new field names
3. **Zero User Impact**: No existing preferences to migrate
4. **Low Test Update Cost**: Only unit tests - no integration/e2e tests yet
5. **Document Sync**: Design docs are still draft (status: "draft"), errata is trivial

**If Deferred**:
- UI components (20+ files) will need updates
- Settings schema freeze creates backward compatibility constraint
- Test suite grows exponentially harder to update
- Migration path becomes required (breaking change vs clean slate)

## 8. Compound Key Format Change

### Current Pattern
```typescript
const key = `${f.groupId}:${f.dataItemId}`;  // Line 57, projection.ts
```

### Proposed Pattern
```typescript
const key = `${f.frameId}:${f.fieldId}`;
```

**Impact**: Single line change in `projectChartSeries()` function

**Downstream Effects**: None - key is internal to display projection logic

---

## 9. Risk Assessment

### Technical Risk: **LOW** ✓
- Pure mechanical rename (find-replace with semantic review)
- Zero runtime behavior changes
- Zero API surface changes (internal types only)
- Isolated to single feature

### Schedule Risk: **MINIMAL** ✓
- Estimated effort: 2-3 hours
  - Core files: 1 hour (5 files, systematic rename)
  - Tests/fixtures: 1 hour (2 files, data update)
  - Docs: 30 min (3 files, errata notes)
  - Validation: 30 min (build + test suite)

### Future Debt Risk: **ELIMINATED** ✓
- Resolves identity terminology divergence **before** it propagates
- Avoids "grandfathered naming" technical debt
- Aligns display with frame feature's established pattern

---

## 10. Final Recommendation

### **APPROVE - Execute Immediately Before UI Implementation**

**Justification**:
1. **Perfect Timing Window**: Core complete, UI not started, schema not frozen
2. **Zero Breaking Changes**: No user data, no frozen APIs, no cross-feature coupling
3. **Semantic Clarity**: `frameId`/`fieldId` aligns with frame feature and reduces cognitive load
4. **Document Debt**: Design docs are draft-status - errata is trivial now, painful later
5. **Test Coverage**: Unit tests in place, will catch any regressions immediately

**Blocking Check**: Run `pnpm -C rewrite test --run` post-change to validate mechanical rename correctness.

**Open Question Resolution**: This change **partially resolves** the "数据身份标识统一" question (brainstorm.md line 634) by standardizing display feature's terminology to match frame feature. Remaining work is storage/channel identity unification (separate concern).

标识字段\n\n| Feature | 组标识 | 项标识 | 复合键格式 | 来源 |\n|---------|--------|--------|-----------|------|\n| **Frame** | `FrameAsset.id` (frameId) | `FrameFieldDefinition.id` (fieldId) | `frameId:fieldId` | 事实源头 |\n| **Receive** | `frameId` | `fieldId` | `frameId:fieldId` (fieldKey 函数) | 直接透传 frame |\n| **Display** | `groupId` | `dataItemId` | `groupId:dataItemId` | bridge 重命名 |\n| **Status** | `groupId` | `dataItemId` | — | 同 display 模式，未接入 runtime |\n| **Storage** | `channel` | `StorageRecordField.key` | — | 独立模型（按连接源分组） |\n\n### 1.2 映射转换点\n\n共 5 个实际映射点：\n\n| # | 位置 | 输入→输出 | 复杂度 | 信息损失 | 语义理由 |\n|---|------|----------|--------|---------|---------|\n| 1 | `connection-to-receive.ts` | `connectionId → sourceId` | 1/5 | 无 | **有理由**：源抽象层，分离传输层与解析层 |\n| 2 | `receive-display-bridge.ts:16` | channel` | 1/5 | 无 | **有理由**：按连接源分组，storage 语义 |\n| 5 | `receive-storage-bridge.ts:15` | `fieldName → key` | 1/5 | 无 | **有理由**：key-value 存储语义，人可读 |\n\n### 1.3 代码证据\n\n**无理由的 display 重命名** (`receive-display-bridge.ts:15-22`):\n```typescript\nfields.push({\n  groupId: f.frameId,        // frameId 原样赋值给 groupId\n  dataItemId: f.fieldId,     // fieldId 原样赋值给 dataItemId\n  fieldName: f.fieldName,\n  value: f.value,\n  displayValue: f.displayValue,\n  updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,\n});\n```\n\n**有理由的 storage 映射** (`receive-storage-bridge.ts:9-18`):\n```typescript\nreturn {\n  // channel = 连接源标识，不是帧标识\n  channel: outcome.input.source.sourceId,\n  fields: outcome.fields.map((f) => ({\n    key: f.fieldName,        // 用人可读名称做 key-value 存储\n    value: f.value ?? null,\n  })),\n};\n```\n\n**Status 同样的重命名模式** (`status/core/types.ts:25-27`):\n```typescript\nexport interface ReceiveFieldMaterial {\n  readonly groupId: string;    // = frameId\n  readonly dataItemId: string; // = fieldId\n  readonly value: unknown;\n  readonly receivedAt?: string;\n}\n```\n\n**已有 fieldKey 工具** (`receive/state/receive-state.ts:120-122`):\n```typescript\nfunction fieldKey(field: Pick<ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot, 'frameId' | 'fieldId'>): string {\n  return `${field.frameId}:${field.fieldId}`;\n}\n```\n\n### 1.4 Storage 独立模型的合理性\n\nStorage 的 `channel` 按连接源分组（不是按帧），`key` 用人可读名称（不是 fieldId）。\n这是有意为之的设计选择：\n- `channel` 回答\"数据从哪个连接来\"\n- `key` 回答\"这个字段的显示名是什么\"\n- Storage 不知道帧定义，不依赖 frame feature\n\n这与 frame/receive/display 的标识模型是**正交**的——不同的抽象维度。\n\n---\n\n## 2. 决策层：推荐方案\n\n### 2.1 结论\n\n**保留两种标识模型，统一命名**：\n\n1. **定义标识**：`frameId` + `fieldId`，frame/receive/display/status 统一使用\n2. **存储标识**：`channel` + `key`，storage 独立使用，映射在 bridge 中显式完成\n\n具体改动：\n- Display: `groupId` → `frameId`，`dataItemId` → `fieldId`\n- Status: `groupId` → `frameId`，`dataItemId` → `fieldId`\n- Storage: **不动**\n- Receive/Frame: **不动**\n\n### 2.2 为什么不是全统一（方案 A）\n\n方案 A 把 storage 的 `channel` 也改成 `frameId`，`key` 改成 `fieldId`。\n\n代价：\n- Storage 丢失\"按连接源分组\"能力（多连接收同一帧时无法区分来源）\n- Storage field key 需要复合键（不同帧的 `fieldId` 可能相同）\n- 破坏 storage 设计的核心抽象（storage 不依赖 frame）\n\n判断：storage 的独立标识是有意的设计，不是设计债务。统一它的代价大于收益。\n\n### 2.3 为什么不是双标识（方案 B）\n\n方案 B 给 storage record 加 `frameId`/`fieldId` 作为可选引用。\n\n代价：\n- 引入\"什么时候可选、什么时候必选\"的复杂规则\n- 对当前 MVP 来说是过早优化——目前没有 feature 需要从 storage record 反查帧定义\n- Storage 设计文档明确说\"storage 不定义帧语义\"\n\n判断：可以作为**未来扩展**（report/history feature 需要时再加），不是当前决策。\n\n### 2.4 为什么包含 Status\n\nStatus 使用与 display 完全相同的 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 模式，语义相同（接收帧绑定）。\nStatus 还没有接入 runtime（无 bridge），改零成本。如果不改，会形成 display 用 `frameId`/`fieldId`、\nstatus 用 `groupId`/`dataItemId` 的不一致，后续需要第二次改名。\n\n---\n\n## 3. 自检层\n\n### 3.1 R1：假设验证\n\n| 假设 | 验证方式 | 结论 > AGENT

Brainstorm 文档已写入 `codestable/features/rewrite-display/display-identity-brainstorm.md`。

---

## 结论摘要

### 当前 5 个映射转换点

| # | 映射 | 有无语义理由 |
|---|------|------------|
| 1 | connection→receive: `connectionId→sourceId` | 有（源抽象） |
| 2 | receive→display: `frameId→groupId` | **无**（纯重命名） |
| 3 | receive→display: `fieldId→dataItemId` | **无**（纯重命名） |
| 4 | receive→storage: `sourceId→channel` | 有（按连接源分组） |
| 5 | receive→storage: `fieldName→key` | 有（人可读 key-value） |

### 推荐：部分统一（Scheme C）

- **Display + Status**：`groupId→frameId`，`dataItemId→fieldId`（消除无理由重命名）
- **Storage**：保持 `channel` + `key` 不变（不同的抽象维度，有语义理由）
- 两种标识模型并存：定义标识（`frameId+fieldId`）和存储标识（`channel+key`）

### 为什么不全统一到 storage

Storage 的 `channel` 按连接源分组（多连接收同一帧时需区分来源），`key` 用人可读名称。这是有意设计，不是设计债务。强行统一会破坏 storage 的核心抽象。

### 实施时机：现在

- Display UI 未实现、Settings schema 未冻结
- Status 未接入 runtime
- 零用户数据、零迁移成本
- 影响面：15 个代码文件 + 3 个设计文档，约 2-3 小时

需要你确认这个方向，然后可以进入实施。
